ROOT_get_in_the_competition_for_China_reunification = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = Participate_in_the_competition_for_China_reunification
	set_country_flag = Participate_in_the_competition_for_China_reunification
	hidden_effect = {
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = is_whole_chinese_state_area
				not = { is_core_of = ROOT }
			}
			add_claim_by = ROOT
		}
		ROOT = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						has_state_flag = Central_Plains_state_area
						has_state_flag = huguang_state_area
						has_state_flag = mingan_state_area
						has_state_flag = liangguang_state_area
						has_state_flag = yungui_state_area
						has_state_flag = jiangzhe_state_area
						has_state_flag = sichuan_state_area
						has_state_flag = Xibei_state_area
						has_state_flag = MAN_state_area
					}
					not = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				}
				enable_state_integration_50_compliance = yes
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						has_state_flag = SIK_state_area
						has_state_flag = menggu_start_state_area
						has_state_flag = xizang_state_area
					}
					not = { is_core_of = ROOT }
					not = {
						or = {
							state = 612
							state = 621
						}
					}
				}
				enable_state_integration_80_compliance = yes
			}
			326 = { enable_state_integration_50_compliance = yes }  ###香港
			524 = { enable_state_integration_50_compliance = yes }  ###台湾
			729 = { enable_state_integration_50_compliance = yes }  ###澳门
			743 = { enable_state_integration_50_compliance = yes }  ###上海
			1187 = { enable_state_integration_50_compliance = yes }  ###上海租界
			612 = { enable_state_integration_50_compliance = yes }  ###锡林郭勒
			621 = { enable_state_integration_50_compliance = yes }  ###乌兰察布
			###外东北
			561 = { enable_state_integration_80_compliance = yes }
			657 = { enable_state_integration_80_compliance = yes }
			560 = { enable_state_integration_80_compliance = yes }
			409 = { enable_state_integration_80_compliance = yes }
			408 = { enable_state_integration_80_compliance = yes }
			537 = { enable_state_integration_80_compliance = yes }
			###外东北
		}
	}
}

CHI_Stable_society = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = CHI_Stable_society_tt
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_Political_instability_4
		}
		remove_ideas = CHI_Political_instability_4
		set_country_flag = Stable_society_done
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_Political_instability_3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = CHI_Political_instability_3
			add_idea = CHI_Political_instability_4
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_Political_instability_2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = CHI_Political_instability_2
			add_idea = CHI_Political_instability_3
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_Political_instability_1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = CHI_Political_instability_1
			add_idea = CHI_Political_instability_2
		}
	}
}

CHI_Famine_relief = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = CHI_Famine_relief_tt
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_famine_5
		}
		remove_ideas = CHI_famine_5
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_famine_4
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = CHI_famine_4
			add_idea = CHI_famine_5
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_famine_3
		}

			add_idea = CHI_famine_4

			remove_idea = CHI_famine_2
			add_idea = CHI_famine_3

		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = CHI_famine_1
			add_idea = CHI_famine_2
		}
	}
}

# 保持原政府傀儡-中国军阀
CHI_puppet_released_tag_and_old_govern = {
 ##原政府变量
	var:tag_to_release = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { old_govern_democratic_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@democratic }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = old_govern_democratic_popularity
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			set_variable = { old_govern_communism_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@communism }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = old_govern_communism_popularity
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			set_variable = { old_govern_neutrality_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@neutrality }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = old_govern_neutrality_popularity
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			set_variable = { old_govern_fascism_popularity = ROOT.party_popularity_100@fascism }
			clamp_variable = {
				var = old_govern_fascism_popularity
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = democratic
				}
				set_country_flag = old_govern_is_democratic_flag
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = neutrality
				}
				set_country_flag = old_govern_is_neutral_flag
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = communism
				}
				set_country_flag = old_govern_is_communistic_flag
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = fascism
				}
				set_country_flag = old_govern_is_fascist_flag
			}
		}
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = puppet_released_tag_and_old_govern_tt
 ##加入战争
	var:tag_to_release = {
		every_enemy_country = {
			limit = { is_ally_of_ROOT = no }
			ROOT = { add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = PREV.PREV enemy = PREV hostility_reason = asked_to_join } }
		}
	}
 ##傀儡等级
	set_autonomy = {
		target = var:tag_to_release
		autonomous_state = China_Provinces
	}
 ##设置原政府
	var:tag_to_release = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_popularities = {
				democratic = old_govern_democratic_popularity
				fascism = old_govern_fascism_popularity
				communism = old_govern_communism_popularity
				neutrality = old_govern_neutrality_popularity
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = old_govern_is_democratic_flag
				}
				set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = old_govern_is_neutral_flag
				}
				set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = old_govern_is_communistic_flag
				}
				set_politics = { ruling_party = communism }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = old_govern_is_fascist_flag
				}
				set_politics = { ruling_party = fascism }
			}
		}
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
 ##设置修饰性tag
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { tag = CHI } }
			add_ideas = CTS_Imperial_Province_idea
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SIC }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIC_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = FUJ }
				set_cosmetic_tag = FUJ_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = JSZ }
				set_cosmetic_tag = JSZ_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SHD }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHD_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = WUH }
				set_cosmetic_tag = WUH_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = YUM }
				set_cosmetic_tag = YUM_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = ROC }
				set_cosmetic_tag = ROC_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = XSM }
				set_cosmetic_tag = XSM_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = YUN }
				set_cosmetic_tag = YUN_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = MON }
				set_cosmetic_tag = MON_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SIK }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIK_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = TIB }
				set_cosmetic_tag = TIB_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = GXC }
				set_cosmetic_tag = ROC_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = MAN }
				set_cosmetic_tag = MAN_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = RYU }
				set_cosmetic_tag = RYU_CHI
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SHX }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHX_CHI
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { tag = ROC } }
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SIC }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIC_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = FUJ }
				set_cosmetic_tag = FUJ_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = JSZ }
				set_cosmetic_tag = JSZ_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SHD }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHD_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = WUH }
				set_cosmetic_tag = WUH_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = YUM }
				set_cosmetic_tag = YUM_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = ROC }
				set_cosmetic_tag = ROC_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = XSM }
				set_cosmetic_tag = XSM_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = YUN }
				set_cosmetic_tag = YUN_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = MON }
				set_cosmetic_tag = MON_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SIK }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIK_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = TIB }
				set_cosmetic_tag = TIB_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = GXC }
				set_cosmetic_tag = GXC_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = MAN }
				set_cosmetic_tag = MAN_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = RYU }
				set_cosmetic_tag = RYU_ROC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SHX }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHX_ROC
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { tag = PRC } }
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SIC }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIC_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = FUJ }
				set_cosmetic_tag = FUJ_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = JSZ }
				set_cosmetic_tag = JSZ_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SHD }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHD_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = WUH }
				set_cosmetic_tag = WUH_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = YUM }
				set_cosmetic_tag = YUM_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = ROC }
				set_cosmetic_tag = ROC_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = XSM }
				set_cosmetic_tag = XSM_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = YUN }
				set_cosmetic_tag = YUN_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = MON }
				set_cosmetic_tag = MON_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SIK }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SIK_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = TIB }
				set_cosmetic_tag = TIB_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = GXC }
				set_cosmetic_tag = GXC_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = MAN }
				set_cosmetic_tag = MAN_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = RYU }
				set_cosmetic_tag = RYU_PRC
			}
			if = {
				limit = { tag = SHX }
				set_cosmetic_tag = SHX_PRC
			}
		}
	}
 ##启动整合决议
	if = {
		limit = { THIS = { tag = PRC } }
		activate_targeted_decision = {
			target = var:tag_to_release
			decision = PRC_integration_FROM
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { THIS = { tag = ROC } }
		activate_targeted_decision = {
			target = var:tag_to_release
			decision = ROC_integration_FROM
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { THIS = { tag = CHI } }
		activate_targeted_decision = {
			target = var:tag_to_release
			decision = CHI_integration_FROM
		}
	}
	else = {
		activate_targeted_decision = {
			target = var:tag_to_release
			decision = CHI_generic_integration_FROM
		}
	}
}

#落日
JAP_peace_with_unit_font = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = JAP_Fading_Sun }
			}
			set_global_flag = JAP_Fading_Sun
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = JAP_zhongrizhanzhengshibai }
			}
			set_global_flag = JAP_zhongrizhanzhengshibai
			JAP = {
				#清除了残余战争借口
				every_enemy_country = {
					JAP = {
						remove_wargoal = {
							type = all
							target = PREV
						}
					}
				}
				#附庸国独立（中国及朝鲜）
				if = {
					limit = { exists = yes }
					every_country = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								is_guaranteed_by = PREV
								is_subject_of = PREV
								has_non_aggression_pact_with = PREV
								has_military_access_to = PREV
								gives_military_access_to = PREV
							}
							or = {
								is_chinese_country = yes
								tag = KOR
							}
						}
						diplomatic_relation = {
							country = JAP
							relation = military_access
							active = no
						}
						JAP = {
							if = {
								limit = { has_subject = PREV }
								set_autonomy = {
									target = PREV
									autonomy_state = autonomy_free
								}
							}
							diplomatic_relation = {
								country = PREV
								relation = guarantee
								active = no
							}
							diplomatic_relation = {
								country = PREV
								relation = non_aggression_pact
								active = no
							}
							diplomatic_relation = {
								country = PREV
								relation = military_access
								active = no
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			#清除本土外的核心与宣称（国区）
			every_state = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = is_whole_chinese_state_area }
				remove_core_of = JAP
				remove_claim_by = JAP
			}
			#地块转移
			if = {	#如果苏联参战，朝鲜给苏联	
				limit = {
					JAP = {
						has_war_with = SOV
					}
				}
				SOV = {
					annex_country = { target = KOR }
				}
			}
			every_state = { #如果苏联参战，朝鲜给苏联
				limit = {
					owner = { tag = ROOT }
					region = 191
				}
				if = {
					limit = { 
						SOV = {	has_war_with = ROOT	}
					}
					SOV = {
						transfer_state = PREV
					}
				}
				
			}

			every_state = { #中国领土，谁控制给谁
				limit = {
					owner = { 
						or = {
							is_ally_with = ROOT
							is_in_faction_with = ROOT
							is_subject_of = ROOT
							tag = ROOT
						}						 
					}
					or = {
						has_state_flag = is_whole_chinese_state_area
						region = 191
					}
					controller = {
						is_chinese_country = yes
						has_war_with = ROOT
					}
				}
				controller = {
					if = {
						limit = { is_subject = yes }
						overlord = { transfer_state = PREV.PREV }
					}
					else = {
						transfer_state = PREV
					}
				}
			}

			every_state = { #樱花控制的中国领土归还给阵营领袖
				limit = {
					owner = { 
						or = {
							is_ally_with = ROOT
							is_in_faction_with = ROOT
							is_subject_of = ROOT
							tag = ROOT
						}							 
					}
					or = {
						has_state_flag = is_whole_chinese_state_area
						region = 191
					}
				}
				ROOT = {
					every_enemy_country = {
						limit = {
							is_chinese_country = yes
							is_faction_leader = yes
						}
						transfer_state = PREV.PREV
					}
				}
				
			}

			#越南
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						owner = { tag = ROOT }
						controller = {
							or = {
								tag = FROM
								is_ally_with = FROM
							}
						}
					}
					controller = {
						transfer_state = PREV
					}
				}

			#中国盟友-离开阵营
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = JAP
					is_chinese_country = yes
				}
				leave_faction = yes
				every_enemy_country = {
					limit = { is_chinese_country = no }
					white_peace = { tag = PREV }
				}
			}

			#已投降的盟友-离开阵营
			if = {
				limit = {
					or = {
						FROM = { is_ai = no }
						any_enemy_country = {
							is_ai = no
							is_in_faction_with = FROM
						}
						and = {
							not = { has_war_with = ENG }
							not = { has_war_with = SOV }
						}
					}
				}
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						is_in_faction_with = JAP
						is_chinese_country = no
						has_capitulated = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { is_subject = yes }
						end_puppet_this = yes
					}
					leave_faction = yes
				}
			}

			#白和
			ROOT = {
				white_peace = {
					tag = FROM
					message = ANTI_JAP_WAR_WIN
				}
				every_enemy_country = {
					limit = { is_in_faction_with = FROM }
					white_peace = {
						tag = ROOT
						message = ANTI_JAP_WAR_WIN
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

						}

						NOT = {
							controller = {
								or = {
									and = {
										or = {

											has_country_flag = PRC_country
										}
										is_subject = no
									}
									is_subject_of = PRC

		}

##中原王朝危机危机度
Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_Var_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		clamp_variable = {
			var = Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_Var
			max = 100
			min = 0
		}
		set_variable = { Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_progress_var = 100 }
		subtract_from_variable = { Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_progress_var = Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_Var }
		set_variable = { Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_progress_fire_var = Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_progress_var }
		multiply_variable = { Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_progress_fire_var = 3.68 }
		add_to_variable = { Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_progress_fire_var = 51 }
		set_variable = { CCDM_political_power_factor = Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_Var }
		divide_variable = { CCDM_political_power_factor = -300 }
		set_variable = { CCDM_industrial_capacity_factory = CCDM_political_power_factor }
		divide_variable = { CCDM_industrial_capacity_factory = 2 }
		set_variable = { CCDM_consumer_goods_factor = Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_Var }
		divide_variable = { CCDM_consumer_goods_factor = 200 }
		set_variable = { CCDM_stability_factor = CCDM_consumer_goods_factor }
		multiply_variable = { CCDM_stability_factor = -1 }
		set_variable = { CCDM_war_support_factor = CCDM_stability_factor }
		set_variable = { CCDM_compliance_growth = Crisis_of_CHI_Dynasty_Var }
		divide_variable = { CCDM_compliance_growth = -1000 }
	}
}

##军改
CHI_army_reform_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_1
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_1
			add_idea = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_2
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_2
			add_idea = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_3
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_3
		}
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_3
			add_idea = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_4
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_4
		}
		remove_ideas = CHI_Corrupt_and_incompetent_army_4
		set_country_flag = army_reform_done
	}
}

##中国派系影响力
CHI_faction_influence_calculate = {
	clamp_variable = { var = CPC_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = PWDP_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = Cadets_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = ZhiGong_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = KMT_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = CLU_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = GongChe_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = Youth_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = North_Army_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = South_Army_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = Honour_Emperor_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	clamp_variable = { var = Innovation_Alliance_faction_influence_var min = 0 }
	set_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 1 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 1 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = CPC_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 2 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 2 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = PWDP_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 3 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 3 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = Cadets_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 4 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 4 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = ZhiGong_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 5 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 5 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = KMT_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 6 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 6 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = CLU_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 7 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 7 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = GongChe_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 8 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 8 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = Youth_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 9 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 9 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = North_Army_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 10 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 10 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = South_Army_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 11 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 11 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = Honour_Emperor_faction_influence_var }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 12 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 12 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { CHI_faction_influence_total = Innovation_Alliance_faction_influence_var }
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 1 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 1 }
			}
		}
		divide_variable = { CPC_faction_influence_var = CHI_faction_influence_total }
		set_variable = { CPC_faction_seat = CPC_faction_influence_var }
		multiply_variable = { CPC_faction_seat = 561 }
		round_variable = CPC_faction_seat
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { CPC_faction_seat = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 2 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 2 }
			}
		}
		divide_variable = { PWDP_faction_influence_var = CHI_faction_influence_total }
		set_variable = { PWDP_faction_seat = PWDP_faction_influence_var }
		multiply_variable = { PWDP_faction_seat = 561 }
		round_variable = PWDP_faction_seat
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { PWDP_faction_seat = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 3 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 3 }
			}
		}
		divide_variable = { Cadets_faction_influence_var = CHI_faction_influence_total }
		set_variable = { Cadets_faction_seat = Cadets_faction_influence_var }
		multiply_variable = { Cadets_faction_seat = 561 }
		round_variable = Cadets_faction_seat
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { Cadets_faction_seat = 0 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 4 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 4 }
			}
		}
		divide_variable = { ZhiGong_faction_influence_var = CHI_faction_influence_total }
		set_variable = { ZhiGong_faction_seat = ZhiGong_faction_influence_var }
		multiply_variable = { ZhiGong_faction_seat = 561 }
		round_variable = ZhiGong_faction_seat
	}
	else = {

	if = {

				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 5 }

		}
		divide_variable = { KMT_faction_influence_var = CHI_faction_influence_total }

		round_variable = KMT_faction_seat
	}

	if = {

				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 6 }
			}
		}
		divide_variable = { CLU_faction_influence_var = CHI_faction_influence_total }
		set_variable = { CLU_faction_seat = CLU_faction_influence_var }
		multiply_variable = { CLU_faction_seat = 561 }
		round_variable = CLU_faction_seat
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { CLU_faction_seat = 0 }
	}
	if = {

			or = {
				is_in_array = { CHI_main_party_array = 7 }
				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 7 }
			}
		}
		divide_variable = { GongChe_faction_influence_var = CHI_faction_influence_total }
		set_variable = { GongChe_faction_seat = GongChe_faction_influence_var }

				is_in_array = { CHI_minor_party_array = 8 }

		divide_variable = { Youth_faction_influence_var = CHI_faction_influence_total }
		set_variable = { Youth_faction_seat = Youth_faction_influence_var }

	subtract_from_variable = { Innovation_Alliance_faction_seat = Youth_faction_seat }

	subtract_from_variable = { Innovation_Alliance_faction_seat = South_Army_faction_seat }

###  天朝朝贡体系计算  ###
China_tribute_system_effect = {
	###  统计经济阵营成员数量
	clear_array = global.China_tribute_system_member  ##成员所在的数组
	clear_array = global.CTS_Imperial_Province_member
	clear_array = global.CTS_Imperial_Vassal_member
	clear_array = global.CTS_Imperial_Tributary_member
	set_variable = { global.num_of_China_tribute_system = 0 }  ##成员数量变量
	every_country = {
		limit = { check_variable = { THIS.economy_faction_var = 7 } }
		add_to_array = { global.China_tribute_system_member = THIS }
		add_to_variable = { global.num_of_China_tribute_system = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_autonomy_state = China_Provinces }
			add_to_array = { global.CTS_Imperial_Province_member = THIS }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_autonomy_state = China_Domain }
			add_to_array = { global.CTS_Imperial_Province_member = THIS }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_autonomy_state = China_Vassal }
			add_to_array = { global.CTS_Imperial_Vassal_member = THIS }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_subject = no
				not = { tag = CHI }
			}
			add_to_array = { global.CTS_Imperial_Tributary_member = THIS }
		}
	}
	###  计算动态阵营buff（如果有）
	China_tribute_system_leader_modifier_effect = yes
	###  刷新经济阵营GDP
	compute_global_gdp = yes
}

China_tribute_system_leader_modifier_effect = {
	set_variable = { global.China_tribute_system_leader_research_speed_factor = global.economy_faction_GDP_ratio^7 }
	set_variable = { global.China_tribute_system_leader_global_building_slots_factor = global.economy_faction_GDP_ratio^7 }
	set_variable = { global.China_tribute_system_leader_production_speed_buildings_factor = global.economy_faction_GDP_ratio^7 }
	set_variable = { global.China_tribute_system_leader_power_balance_weekly = global.economy_faction_GDP_ratio^7 }
	multiply_variable = { global.China_tribute_system_leader_research_speed_factor = 0.25 }
	multiply_variable = { global.China_tribute_system_leader_global_building_slots_factor = 0.5 }
	multiply_variable = { global.China_tribute_system_leader_production_speed_buildings_factor = 0.5 }
	multiply_variable = { global.China_tribute_system_leader_power_balance_weekly = 0.02 }
}

CHI_dismate_Build_chinese_united_font_effect = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				is_in_faction_with = ROOT
				tag = ROOT
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_array = { global.former_china_unit_font_members = THIS }
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = china_generic.019
				days = 3
			}
		}
	}
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.former_china_unit_font_members
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.former_china_unit_font_members
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { tag = PREV }
				}
				give_military_access = PREV
			}
		}
	}
	faction_leader = { dismantle_faction = yes }
	set_global_flag = dismate_Build_chinese_united_font

	hidden_effect = { every_country = { mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes } }
}

CHI_send_to_Re_education_effect = {#送去劳改
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_advisor_role = {
			slot = high_command
		}
		retire = yes
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = CHI_send_to_Re_education_effect_TT
}

PRC_national_construction_state_index_calculate = {#省份指数计算
	set_variable = { PRC.national_industrial_index = 0 }
	set_variable = { PRC.national_light_industrial_index = 0 }
	set_variable = { PRC.national_agricultural_index = 0 }
	set_variable = { PRC.national_construction_num_of_state = 0 }
	every_controlled_state = {  ##安徽
		limit = {
			is_fully_controlled_by = PRC
			or = {
				state = 1050
				state = 606
				state = 1014
			}
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = ROOT.anhui_industrial_index min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = ROOT.anhui_light_industrial_index min = 0 max = 100 }
		clamp_variable = { var = ROOT.anhui_agricultural_index min = 0 max = 100 }
		add_to_variable = { PRC.national_construction_num_of_state = 1 }
		add_to_variable = { PRC.national_industrial_index = ROOT.anhui_industrial_index }
		add_to_variable = { PRC.national_light_industrial_index = ROOT.anhui_light_industrial_index }
		add_to_variable = { PRC.national_agricultural_index = ROOT.anhui_agricultural_index }
		set_variable = { ROOT.anhui_PCDM_state_resources_factor = ROOT.anhui_industrial_index }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.anhui_PCDM_state_resources_factor = 400 }
		set_variable = { ROOT.anhui_PCDM_local_building_slots_factor = ROOT.anhui_light_industrial_index }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.anhui_PCDM_local_building_slots_factor = 200 }
		set_variable = { ROOT.anhui_PCDM_local_supplies = ROOT.anhui_industrial_index }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.anhui_PCDM_local_supplies = 400 }
		set_variable = { ROOT.anhui_PCDM_recruitable_population = ROOT.anhui_industrial_index }
		divide_variable = { ROOT.anhui_PCDM_recruitable_population = 1000 }
	}

	every_controlled_state = {  ##察哈尔

			is_fully_controlled_by = PRC
